# Any mod, any version: what is not there yet

Written with main's jar scoreboard (scoreboard/all.json on the generated branch) at 6b2ac399e2, run https://github.com/Noisemaker111/minecraft-modding-tools/actions/runs/46033387000. Do not edit by hand.

The 150 most-downloaded mods of each loader's most-used release, each ported as a jar to every other release the loader has, forward and back, and linked against it (classes, mixins, access transformers, data). It says the port links, not that it runs: the game proof is `tools/cloud-run load`.

**9 of 300 mods go to any version (their ported jar links on every release): Fabric 3 of 150 from 1.20.1 to all 43 other releases (7 reach every newer one, 3 every older one); Neoforge 6 of 150 from 1.21.1 to all 21 other releases (16 reach every newer one, 8 every older one)**

**8,524 breaks left: each type of break holding a mod on some release, one per mod; a type handled on every release removes one per mod it held**

## What removes the most breaks

Types of break, by the mods each holds on some release: a way to handle the type on every release removes that many breaks left. *Frees* counts the mods it alone keeps from every release.
